 // in bodies\n    std::vector<int> counts3, displs3;  // in doubles (3 per body)\n    std::vector<int> counts6, displs6;  // in doubles (6 per body)\n    int lo = 0, hi = 0;\n\n    Decomp(int n, int r, int s) : rank(r), size(s), counts(s), displs(s), counts3(s), displs3(s), counts6(s), displs6(s) {\n        const int base = n / s, rem = n % s;\n        int off = 0;\n        for (int p = 0; p < s; ++p) {\n            counts[p] = base + (p < rem ? 1 : 0);\n            displs[p] = off;\n            counts3[p] = 3 * counts[p];\n            displs3[p] = 3 * off;\n            counts6[p] = 6 * counts[p];\n            displs6[p] = 6 * off;\n            off += counts[p];\n        }\n        lo = displs[rank];\n        hi = lo + counts[rank];\n    }\n};\n\nconstexpr int TILE = 8;\n\n// Computes the force on local bodies [lo, hi) from all n bodies and updates their velocity.\n// The per-body summation order over j matches the sequential code exactly; vectorization\n// is done across i (several target bodies at once), which keeps results bit-identical.\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict pos, double* __restrict vel, int lo, int hi, int n) {\n    int i = lo;\n    for (; i + TILE <= hi; i += TILE) {\n        double xi[TILE], yi[TILE], zi[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n            xi[k] = pos[3 * (i + k) + 0];\n            yi[k] = pos[3 * (i + k) + 1];\n            zi[k] = pos[3 * (i + k) + 2];\n            Fx[k] = 0.0;\n            Fy[k] = 0.0;\n            Fz[k] = 0.0;\n        }\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n            for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n            const int li = i + k - lo;\n            vel[3 * li + 0] += DT * Fx[k];\n            vel[3 * li + 1] += DT * Fy[k];\n            vel[3 * li + 2] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n    for (; i < hi; ++i) {\n        const double xi = pos[3 * i + 0], yi = pos[3 * i + 1], zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        const int li = i - lo;\n        vel[3 * li + 0] += DT * Fx;\n        vel[3 * li + 1] += DT * Fy;\n        vel[3 * li + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Advances local bodies' positions (in the global position array) using local velocities.\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict pos, const double* __restrict vel, int lo, int hi) {\n    const int m = 3 * (hi - lo);\n    double* p = pos + 3 * lo;\n    for (int k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n        p[k] += vel[k] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of rows i in [lo, hi), each row is i < j < n.\ndouble computePotentialEnergyPart(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, size_t i) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n        const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n        const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n        const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new_kernel+src[end:]\nopen('nbody.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1434,"cache_read_input_tokens":16326,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1434},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7e3ea014-6bc0-481f-b407-debb38628ca8","uuid":"6b1232b7-a234-4174-95ae-464dbb082d74","timestamp":"2026-10-04T17:12:06.297Z","request_id":"req_011CfhZzPwiuxee8qXspBs2Q"}

MPI_Reduce(&local, &potential, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + \n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + \n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n    return energy + int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);\n    const bool root = (rank == 0);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n    \n    // Initialize bodies (deterministic, so every rank generates the same set)\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    const Decomp d(numBodies, rank, size);\n    std::vector<double> pos(3 * (size_t)numBodies);\n    std::vector<double> vel(3 * (size_t)(d.hi - d.lo));\n    for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {\n        pos[3 * i + 0] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n    }\n    for (int i = d.lo; i < d.hi; ++i) {\n        const int li = i - d.lo;\n        vel[3 * li + 0] = bodies[i].vel.x;\n        vel[3 * li + 1] = bodies[i].vel.y;\n        vel[3 * li + 2] = bodies[i].vel.z;\n    }\n    \n    // Run simulation\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        computeForces(pos.data(), vel.data(), d.lo, d.hi, numBodies);\n        integrateBodies(pos.data(), vel.data(), d.lo, d.hi);\n        // Replicate updated positions to all ranks\n        if (size > 1) {\n            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, pos.data(), d.counts3.data(), d.displs3.data(),\n                           MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    if (root) printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    if (!printResults && !validate) {\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    // Collect final state (positions are already replicated; gather velocities)\n    std::vector<double> allVel(3 * (size_t)numBodies);\n    MPI_Allgatherv(vel.data(), d.counts3[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, allVel.data(), d.counts3.data(), d.displs3.data(),\n                   MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {\n        bodies[i].pos = Vec3(pos[3 * i + 0], pos[3 * i + 1], pos[3 * i + 2]);\n        bodies[i].vel = Vec3(allVel[3 * i + 0], allVel[3 * i + 1], allVel[3 * i + 2]);\n    }\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && root) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    int rc = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (root) printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n        \n        // Every rank holds identical state, so all ranks reach the same verdict\n        if (validateSimulation(bodies, root)) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies, rank, size);\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            if (root) printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            rc = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return rc;\n}\nEOF\npython3 /tmp/edit2.py && cat > {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfhaSCRQpoKx378C92gt4","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The nbody benchmark now runs on MPI, and its output is bit-identical to the original's (same `-r` hash) at every rank count I tried: 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 8, 16, 32, 64 and 128. I changed only `nbody.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt`, and the executable is still `nbody`.\n\n**Performance** (16384 bodies, 10 steps, one 64-core EPYC machine):\n\n| Ranks | Time |\n|---|---|\n| original (serial) | 19928 ms |\n| 1 | 5042 ms |\n| 8 | 636 ms |\n| 64 | 103 ms |\n| 128 | 76 ms |\n\nUp to 64 ranks it scales almost linearly. A single rank is already about 4x faster than the original because the force loop now uses SIMD. I've only run this on one machine, not a real multi-node cluster.\n\n**How it works**\n- **Splitting the work:** each rank owns a contiguous block of bodies and keeps their velocities. Every rank keeps a copy of all positions, and one `MPI_Allgatherv` per step shares the updated ones. Each rank builds the same starting bodies from the fixed seed, so setup needs no communication.\n- **Force loop:** it computes 8 target bodies at once in SIMD but adds up each body's forces in the original order, which is what keeps the results bit-identical. When a block's size isn't a multiple of 8, the last group repeats a body and throws away the extra results, so every body goes through the same code.\n- **Exact rounding:** the original build uses fused multiply-add (FMA) instructions, which round differently from a separate multiply and add. I read the original binary to see exactly which operations were fused, wrote those as explicit `std::fma` calls, and set `-ffp-contract=off` so the compiler can't fuse anything else. Without this, the hash changed whenever the code was vectorized differently.\n- **Compiler flag:** `-fno-math-errno` lets `sqrt` be vectorized. It doesn't change any results, because the value passed to `sqrt` is always positive.\n- **Energy check (`-v`):** the work is split across ranks in balanced contiguous blocks and the partial sums are combined on rank 0.\n- **Output:** only rank 0 prints. Validation returns the same exit code on every rank, and `-h` and unknown options shut MPI down cleanly.\n- **CMake:** uses `find_package(MPI)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`.\n\n**One difference you might notice:** with more than one rank, the \"Final energy\" line can change in the last few printed digits (about 1e-12 relative, e.g. `…787.818356` instead of `…787.814026`), because the partial sums are added in a different order. With one rank it matches the original exactly. The validation result and the body hash are not affected.\n\nEdge cases I checked: more ranks than bodies (8 ranks, 5 bodies), body counts that don't divide evenly (1001 bodies on 6 ranks), `-h`, and an unknown option (exits with code 1)."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":932,"cache_read_input_tokens":43861,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":932},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7e3ea014-6bc0-481f-b407-debb38628ca8","uuid":"27b5cb4e-94c0-469c-bea1-57e498b5fa80","timestamp":"2026-10-04T17:17:49.305Z","request_id":"req_011CfhaSC4a3CNCJvTHoQHCN"}
